2026-10-25T06:05:17.9481552Z text: [BUG] Progress bar prints a new line on every refresh\n\n- [x] I've checked docs and closed issues for possible solutions.\n- [x] I can't find my issue in the FAQ.\n\n**Describe the bug**\n\nRunning my script, every refresh of a `Progress` bar printed a new line instead of updating in place.\n\n```python\nfrom time import sleep\nfrom rich.progress import track\n\nfor _ in track(range(20)):\n    sleep(0.1)\n```\n\n**Update:** solved, my mistake. 2026-10-25T06:05:17.9486999Z title: [BUG] Progress bar prints a new line on every refresh
2026-10-25T06:05:17.9491430Z body: - [x] I've checked docs and closed issues for possible solutions.\n- [x] I can't find my issue in the FAQ.\n\n**Describe the bug**\n\nRunning my script, every refresh of a `Progress` bar printed a new line instead of updating in place.\n\n```python\nfrom time import sleep\nfrom rich.progress import track\n\nfor _ in track(range(20)):\n    sleep(0.1)\n```\n\n**Update:** solved, my mistake.
